# Authored bone-map coverage for #432

Measured 10 October 2026 from origin/main `a42379af9`. Report only. [#432](https://github.com/tompassarelli/smashcraft/issues/432) and reopened [#362](https://github.com/tompassarelli/smashcraft/issues/362) require account b judgments on Warcraft 3.0.0.24268. Every native judgment below is pending; static coverage is not a visual pass. No retarget, Classic clip, gameplay, packaging or client change was made.

Done when: enumerate keyed authored bones for every source sequence of every fighter, validate every rig pair against source and cached stock target, name every source bone without a direct map and every target registration fallback, and put locomotion selections alongside the move tables.

Not required: fixes, renders, new tests or permanent measurement tooling.

The main audit covers 24 fighters, 1853 production/authored sequences (1845 selected by production/victory). Production/victory clips and unselected authored gesture clips are included. Unselected stock sequences are omitted; the full private measurement retains all 2238 source sequences. The two issue-declared Classic exceptions have separate sections: 175 sequences (166 selected). Across all 26 current map modules: 2028 listed moves, 2011 selected, 0 absent required pairs. No stock clip is claimed as newly authored.

## Measurement algorithm and evidence

The denominator is the number of distinct source skeletal transform nodes (MDX `Bones` and `Helpers`) with at least one sequence-local Translation, Rotation or Scaling key in the inclusive clip interval. Held keys count: a held authored pose is still a bone instruction. It is **not** the number of all rig bones, all target bones, all keys, or all mapped pairs. Global-clock channels and bones with no local key in that clip are excluded. The authored model is the build-input manifest's fighter source; the canonical rename changes names only. MDX Helpers count because many anatomical joints, including Rifleman’s feet, are stored as Helpers. Their MDX kinds are listed separately; the authored bone/joint fraction includes both kinds.

The numerator counts each such source bone once if its canonical name appears in a valid rig pair with an existing Definitive bone. Multiple targets for the same source joint count once. `copied` is a bone outside that numerator whose whole-body ancestor chain is copied by the exporter. `missing` is a keyed source bone with neither a direct target nor a copied ancestor role; it has no separately mapped local gesture. Its movement may still affect a mapped descendant's world pose: this count alone establishes no visible defect. For every row, `total = mapped + copied.length + missing.length`.

`varying missing` names the missing bones whose local keyed Vector changes within that clip by more than 1e-6 in any component (quaternion signs aligned by dot product). Empty means held local keys, not that the held pose equals the registered Stand pose. Tangent-only interpolation excursions, skin relevance and deformation quality are outside this count.

A target fallback is intentionally retained by registration: an unmapped target child holds its stock local Stand offset under its parent; a target root holds its stock reference pose and the transferred body frame. Twist ancestors may have stock squash removed. These are **target registration fallbacks**, separate from missing source local mappings. The full per-fighter target list below applies to every move row of that fighter. Unmapped source inventories include inactive/global-only bones as well, so every omitted bone is named without inflating a move denominator.

Source authority (line numbers at measured SHA; abbreviated entries use the same directory as the first full path):

- `tools/animations/canonical-rig.ts:44` validates the Classic names; `:64` applies canonical renaming.
- `tools/animations/hd-retarget.ts:187` resolves each source/target pair and rejects absent/repeated targets; `:201` selects the whole-body ancestor; `:419`–`:439` copies its chain.
- `tools/animations/hd-retarget.ts:306`–`:315` builds constant local registration; `:347`–`:349` uses it for unmapped target nodes; `:465` writes constant local keys for unkeyed target bones.
- `tools/animations/original-clips.ts:15` resolves fighter source paths; `:38` defines global-clock exclusion.
- `tools/animations/hd-models.ts:16` derives production and victory selection; `:120` maps authored source to canonical motion.
- `tools/animations/white-flash-keys.ts:14` derives production clip selection.
- `ts/src/game/presentation/fighterLocomotion.ts:24` selects walk/run/dash; `:43` sets rate from Classic drawn stride. `fighterPose.ts:318`–`:338` selects locomotion, `:423` selects turn/stop, and `:660` selects Illidan-specific motion.

Static command used (scratch remains private; only this numerical report is committed; `report.ts` adds anatomical MDX Helpers to the bone/joint counts and groups equal rows):

`COVERAGE_REPO=~/code/smashcraft/worktrees/retarget-coverage-432 time bun ~/.local/state/smashcraft/coverage-432/measure.ts`

Output: 26 fighter records, 2,238 raw sequence records, 0 absent pairs; 17.53 seconds elapsed, peak RSS 809,148 KiB. Input models were read serially; no renderer, browser, native client, exporter, build or asset resolver was invoked for coverage. Measurement JSON and scratch script: `~/.local/state/smashcraft/coverage-432/`. Source family hashes and exact source/stock byte hashes are recorded per fighter below. Stock cache roots: `~/.local/state/smashcraft/de-anim-362/bodies/stock/`, `bodies-b-final/stock/`, and `~/.local/share/smashcraft-build-inputs/de-last-six-merge/hd2/stock/`; these are extracted stock models, not a new extraction or public art dump.

## Shadow Hunter: first native suspects

All 36 pairs exist and address 21 distinct source joints. The source weapon `Object11` → `@weapon` → `Wep01_R0_0_jnt`, both wrists, shoulders, elbows, hips, knees and ankles are mapped. A missing required weapon/limb pair is therefore not established by this audit. `Bone_Root` and the four motion helpers are intentionally copied. `Beard2`, `Object04`, `Object01` and `Object06` have no direct target; first inspect missing **independent** secondary motion before diagnosing a main-limb mapping failure.

| --- | --- | --- |
| 3 Spell Throw | 21/32; varying unmapped `hero2`, `hero3`, `hero05`, `Beard2`, `Object04`, `Object01`. Also previously reported arm/weapon floor issue in #362; right arm scale 0.745 is source-owned calibration, not a current native pass. | pending |
| 14–30 Recovery family, 40 Drill Down Air, 54–55 Jump Motion | Usually 21/30; `Beard2`, `Object04`, `Object01` vary without a direct target. Root/drill/jump gesture is copied, so absence from pairs does not imply lost body rotation. | pending |
| 57–65 Attack Gesture family; 41–53 Paired Grab family; 31–39 Damage Grid | 21/30; unmapped `Object06`, `Beard2`, `Object04`, `Object01` have held local keys. Prior #362 reports forwardTilt 57, throwBack 48 and Mid Large 36 as investigated. Missing held keys alone cannot rank those moves as failures. | pending |

Rig evidence: `tools/animations/hd-rigs/shadowhunter.ts:5` arm fits; `:26`–`:44` direct bone pairs. The per-move varying list below is exhaustive, including stock clips; no judged failure or native cause is invented.

## Rifleman: one DE gait cycle, first locomotion measurement

Current immutable pool family: `eb85403a744219b505f696e33babc7df8eb832a5782fd663cfd3d9ba3ff55763`. DE timeline SHA-256 `cade42c07069a87e4dc12c1690287ade2fc954ff7a4d5d4c4a14f0e970e281ce`. Both walk and run select source clip 54, `Walk`, interval 148208–149041 ms; production float32 clip endpoints are 148207.992–149041.000 ms. Its mesh was sampled at each game frame in one full cycle, at steady maximum walk/run speed and the production stride-based rate. This is a steady gait measurement, not acceleration, reversal or native acceptance.

Foot sets use opaque/alpha-tested drawn-face vertices whose heaviest SKIN influence is the mapped ankle or its descendants (140 left, 141 right). Every posed vertex uses all four skin weights, not only the dominant weight. Lowest z is the minimum of that foot's visible vertex set, in match world units; contact means `abs(lowest z) <= 2` around model ground z=0. Ground speed is the horizontal centroid displacement of the **same vertex set** over adjacent samples, plus fighter translation. This is a foot centroid proxy; no changing lowest-point index is differentiated. A pair is counted as planted only when both endpoints meet the contact bound. Facing +x, stock match scale 1.0.

Rate authority: `ts/src/game/presentation/fighterLocomotion.ts:26` uses clip 54 for Rifleman's named walk; `:35` reuses it for run; `:45` divides fighter speed by Classic drawn stride. `drawnStrideInfo.ts:5` gives 149.997 units/s for both gaits; `sim/tuning.ts:173`–`:174` gives 9.0/8.4 units per frame (540/504 units per second). `hd-rigs/rifleman.ts:28`–`:29` maps both ankle joints; no required leg pair is absent.

| Gait | Move speed units/s | Playback rate | Cycle game frames | Expected half-cycle offset | Samples with neither foot in ±2 |

| walk | 504.000 | 3.360 | 14.875 | 7.437 | 7/15 |
| run | 540.000 | 3.600 | 13.883 | 6.942 | 6/14 |

| Gait / foot | Lowest z range | Contact frames (all, cycle start=0) | Mean absolute planted centroid ground speed | Ratio to move speed |

Classic comparison at the identical frame/rate samples uses vertices beneath the Classic ankle Helpers (9 per foot) and all actual matrix weights. Its geometry differs, so centroid speed is a proxy in both looks, not a one-to-one vertex match.

| Classic gait / foot | Lowest z range | Contact frames | Mean absolute planted centroid ground speed |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| walk/L | -2.225..22.186 | 0, 4, 5, 11, 12 | 640.250 |
| walk/R | -0.819..20.337 | 4, 5, 6, 7 | 381.284 |
| run/L | -1.371..21.670 | 0, 4, 10, 11, 12 | 347.722 |
| run/R | -1.269..20.337 | 4, 5, 7 | 482.682 |

Cause classification before any fix: **wrong sequence is not established**: both gaits select intended Walk 54. **A missing required leg map is ruled out statically**, while the retarget changes foot contact timing: for example walk right contact 4,5,6,7 in Classic becomes 6,7,12 in DE. **Root/stride and source gait are still candidates**: the rate uses a Classic stride for the DE body, Classic itself has interrupted contacts and nonzero centroid slip, and the keyed source `Bone_Root` Helper has no direct map or copied-chain role in this rig (see Rifleman move row and `hd-retarget.ts:201` / `:419`). Mapped descendant world transforms may still incorporate that helper, so its omission is not proof of lost root motion. These measurements do not isolate the centroid slip to one retarget line. The foot sets require a native lead judgment before choosing a repair.

Native judgment: pending.

## Mountain King: stop/turn/stand diagnosis

DE timeline SHA-256 `34fab8ae511b336aa2ed5176f109afda2c9b52adc684060402330f0532a0cc15`. Intended walk/run/dash selects clip 7 `Walk`; turn selects 27 `Recovery turn`; stop/brake selects 28 `Recovery stop`; idle selects 1 `Stand Ready`. There is no distinct authored `skid` clip in this production table. A still showing trailing dust cannot identify the exact state: no input/pose trace accompanies that screenshot in this measurement. Clip 28 is the strongest folded-forward candidate among these states; **the screenshot's actual sequence remains unconfirmed**.

Spine pitch is `atan2(chest.x - pelvis.x, chest.z - pelvis.z)`, using DE nodes `spine_C0_0_jnt`, `bone_chest` and `bone_head`. The head/hip metric uses those node pivots, not mesh facial pixels. Stop/turn cover all 9 key-frame positions of their 8-frame transition; stand/walk use 31 evenly spaced positions over one cycle. Native skin deformation and camera perspective remain pending.

| DE candidate | Spine pitch degrees | Head-to-pelvis axis pitch degrees | Head above pelvis units |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1: Stand Ready | -37.494..-37.494 | -35.540..-35.540 | 20.106..20.106 |
| 7: Walk | -42.592..-23.204 | -39.489..-13.267 | 19.030..22.223 |
| 27: Recovery turn | -40.599..37.838 | -38.330..36.259 | 16.025..20.106 |
| 28: Recovery stop | -65.507..-37.494 | -63.497..-35.540 | 10.224..20.106 |

| Stock DE sequence, one cycle | Spine pitch degrees | Head-to-pelvis axis pitch degrees | Head above pelvis units |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 0: Stand 1 | 6.535..9.667 | 0.690..4.851 | 26.865..27.012 |
| 1: Stand Ready 1 | 6.535..9.669 | 0.690..4.853 | 26.865..27.012 |
| 3: Stand 3 | 0.444..8.274 | -14.914..3.072 | 24.365..27.049 |
| 4: Stand 4 | -15.720..11.783 | -32.558..3.612 | 20.693..27.012 |
| 7: Walk 1 | 6.500..21.699 | -4.881..17.542 | 25.132..26.503 |

Classic comparison uses head-to-pelvis axis because Classic chest/pelvis pivots do not have the same anatomical ordering as stock DE; their raw chest-to-pelvis angle is not comparable.

| Classic candidate | Head-to-pelvis axis pitch degrees | Head above pelvis units |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1: Stand Ready | -13.424..-13.424 | 17.430..17.430 |
| 7: Walk | -18.766..15.872 | 16.373..18.004 |
| 27: Recovery turn | -16.071..16.147 | 15.902..17.430 |
| 28: Recovery stop | -39.724..-13.424 | 12.324..17.430 |

Static cause category: **retarget rest alignment plus the authored stop lean** is the supported pathway to inspect. No absent chest/head pair was found. `hd-rigs/mountainking.ts:28` maps pelvis/chest/head; every required pair exists. Even transferred Stand Ready has −37.494° spine pitch against stock DE Stand Ready's +6.535..+9.669°. The Classic head-to-pelvis axis at that same idle is −13.424°, against transferred DE −35.540°, so the extra folded pose already exists before stop is selected. `hd-retarget.ts:239`–`:261` registers rest direction, `:354`–`:364` applies the Classic delta and aiming, and `:385` carries the authored body transform. `recovery-clips.ts:38` adds stop lean −20° to −25° and squash 0.12, taking the DE head-to-pelvis axis to −63.497° / only 10.224 units above pelvis at the sampled extreme. Turn also applies yaw and smaller lean (`:37`), which can change the projected spine angle without being a collapse.

This identifies the static registration/stop pathway to inspect, not the native screenshot's exact frame or a visually accepted failure. No map, pitch, source gesture or stock sequence was changed. Native judgment: pending.

Pose command: `COVERAGE_REPO=~/code/smashcraft/worktrees/retarget-coverage-432 time taskset -c 0 bun --smol ~/.local/state/smashcraft/coverage-432/pose.ts`. Final output: 0.86 seconds elapsed, 99% of one CPU, peak RSS 166,540 KiB. It instantiates the existing model pose evaluator but never creates graphics, loads textures, renders an image, launches a client or writes an asset. Private raw numerical frames are in `~/.local/state/smashcraft/coverage-432/pose.json`.

## Per-fighter and per-sequence coverage

`selected=yes` is the current production/victory selection, not a native pass. Each source-missing column names every keyed skeletal node without its own target. Identical coverage tuples are grouped; every clip index, name and production alias remains listed, and every listed move inherits the exact displayed fraction and fallback names. `Hn` expands to the complete named helper list immediately above the table; `Dn` expands to the complete target fallback inventory of that fighter. Both inventories apply only to that fighter. A dash denotes an empty list.

## Lich King and Malfurion: separate exceptions

#432 and #362 declare Classic bodies for these fighters. At measured main, however, `ts/src/game/assets/definitiveFighters.ts:12` includes both; `hd-rigs/lichking.ts:2` uses stock Definitive Death Knight Arthas without his horse, and `hd-rigs/malfurion.ts:2` uses stock no-stag Malfurion. These current maps are measured separately so neither a Classic-policy claim nor missing roster coverage is inferred. This report changes neither source nor exception policy.

## Limits and remaining work

All native judgments remain pending; cached stock hashes are recorded, but their identity against the account b client archive has not been checked here. Other fighters have locomotion sequence coverage, with per-foot pose measurements still pending. The report establishes pair existence, keyed source coverage and named fallback pathways; it does not assign limb-twist, collapse, sliding or weapon-attachment pass/fail. Account b startup/active/end strips and the lead's judgment are the next evidence. The source input and cached stock hashes make this measurement specific to the named main and immutable input families.
